## S001 RMC/include/rmc/file/hdf5/HDF5.hpp:62-128
   62	    class HDF5 {
   63	    public:
   64	      enum : unsigned int {
   65	        /// 文件打开模式：只读。即，打开已有文件
   66	        //
   67	        ReadOnly = HighFive::File::ReadOnly,
   68	
   69	        /// 文件打开模式：修改。即，打开已有文件，并修改其中的内容
   70	        //
   71	        Modify = HighFive::File::ReadWrite,
   72	
   73	        /// 文件打开模式：覆写。即，创建新文件；若已有文件，则使用新文件覆盖旧文件
   74	        //
   75	        OverWrite = HighFive::File::Truncate
   76	      };
   77	
   78	    public:
   79	      /// default constructor
   80	      HDF5() : HDF5("RMC.hdf5", ReadOnly) {}
   81	
   82	      /**
   83	       * @brief constructor
   84	       * @param fileName name of the hdf5 file
   85	       * @param openFlags
   86	       *   how to access the hdf5 file:
   87	       *   1. ReadOnly
   88	       *   2. Modify
   89	       *   3. OverWrite
   90	       */
   91	      HDF5(const std::string &fileName, unsigned openFlags);
   92	
   93	      /// de-constructor
   94	      ~HDF5() {
   95	        if (h5File) {
   96	          delete h5File;
   97	          h5File = nullptr;
   98	        }
   99	      }
  100	
  101	      /**
  102	       * @brief 把HDF5缓存输出到文件中
  103	       */
  104	      void Flush();
  105	
  106	      /**
  107	       * @brief close the HDF5 file
  108	       */
  109	      void Close();
  110	
  111	      /**
  112	       * @brief 写入数据集
  113	       * @note 如果数据集不存在，则直接创建数据集；如果存在，则用新数据集覆盖旧数据集
  114	       * @tparam T 待写数据类型
  115	       * @param[in] datasetPath 数据集的绝对路径
  116	       * @param[in] data 数据
  117	       */
  118	      template<typename T>
  119	      void WriteData(const std::string &datasetPath, const T &data);
  120	
  121	      /**
  122	       * @brief 读取数据
  123	       * @param[in] datasetPath 数据在HDF5文件中的全路径
  124	       * @param[out] data 读取到的数据
  125	       * @note 函数会根据读取到的hdf5数据，自动进行resize类操作
  126	       */
  127	      template<typename T>
  128	      void ReadData(const std::string &datasetPath, T &data);

## S003 RMC/include/rmc/file/hdf5/HDF5_misc.hpp:33-49
   33	    template<typename T>
   34	    inline void HDF5::WriteData(const std::string &datasetPath,
   35	                                const T &data) {
   36	      if (HDF5Tool::ExistPath(datasetPath, *h5File)) {
   37	        DeleteData(datasetPath);
   38	      }
   39	      HighFive::DataSet dataSet =
   40	          HDF5Tool::CreateDataSet(datasetPath, *h5File, data);
   41	    }
   42	
   43	    template<typename T>
   44	    inline void HDF5::ReadData(const std::string &datasetPath,
   45	                               T &data) {
   46	      HighFive::DataSet dataSet =
   47	          HDF5Tool::GetExistDataSet(datasetPath, *h5File);
   48	
   49	      dataSet.read(data);

  329	          for (int i = 0; i < cExternalSource.p_nFixedInitPhoSrcBankCount; ++i) {
  330	            //这里不使用pushinducedparticle函数的原因是没有对应的粒子事件输出;
  331	            cFixedSource.pushInducedParticles(cFixedSource.p_vFixedParticleSrcBank[CDParticleState::Photon], 1,
  332	                                              cExternalSource.p_vFixedInitPhoSrcBank[i].p_dPos,
  333	                                              cExternalSource.p_vFixedInitPhoSrcBank[i].p_dDir,
  334	                                              cExternalSource.p_vFixedInitPhoSrcBank[i].p_dErg,
  335	                                              cExternalSource.p_vFixedInitPhoSrcBank[i].p_dTime,
  336	                                              cExternalSource.p_vFixedInitPhoSrcBank[i].p_dWgt);
  337	          }
  338	          cExternalSource.p_nFixedInitPhoSrcBankCount = 0;
  339	          cExternalSource.p_vFixedInitPhoSrcBank.clear();
  340	        }
  341	        if (cFixedSource.p_nFinishCalculate == 2 || cFixedSource.p_nFinishCalculate == 4) {
  342	          break;
  343	        }
  344	        cFixedSource.p_llCurParNumEachPro++;    // 这个变量是当前模拟粒子数，用于判断是否模拟完\n
  345	        if (CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Neutron) {
  346	          CGeometry.p_OBinaryOut.UpdateParticleTypeAndHistoryNum(0, true);
  347	        } else if (CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Photon) {
  348	          CGeometry.p_OBinaryOut.UpdateParticleTypeAndHistoryNum(1, true);
  349	        }
  350	
  351	# ifdef USE_MPI
  352	        cFixedSource.p_llCurrentPARTICLE = cFixedSource.p_llParNumRestart + OParallel.p_nProcsNum *
  353	                                           cFixedSource.p_llCurParNumEachPro;
  354	        if (OParallel.p_bIsMaster) {
  355	          if (CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Neutron) {
  356	            if (cFixedSource.p_llCurParNumEachPro % 1000 == 0) {
  357	              printf("neutron : %lld\n", cFixedSource.p_llCurrentPARTICLE);
  358	            }
  359	          } else {
  360	            if (cFixedSource.p_llCurParNumEachPro % 1000 == 0) {
  361	              printf("photon : %lld\n", cFixedSource.p_llCurrentPARTICLE);
  362	            }
  363	          }
  364	        }
  365	# else
  366	        cFixedSource.p_llCurrentPARTICLE = cFixedSource.p_llParNumRestart + cFixedSource.p_llCurParNumEachPro;
  367	        if (CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Neutron) {
  368	          if (cFixedSource.p_llCurParNumEachPro % 1000 == 0) { printf("neutron : %lld\n", cFixedSource.p_llCurrentPARTICLE); }
  369	        } else {
  370	          if (cFixedSource.p_llCurParNumEachPro % 1000 == 0) { printf("photon : %lld\n", cFixedSource.p_llCurrentPARTICLE); }
  371	        }
  372	# endif
  373	
  374	        //////////////////  neutron history  /////////////////////////
  375	        for (;;) {
  376	          if (CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Neutron) {
  377	            cFixedSource.TrackHistory(cCalMode, cNeutronTransport, cPhotonTransport, CGeometry,
  378	                                      CMaterial, CAceData, CParticleState, CTally, ORNG, cSampling,
  379	                                      cPerturb);
  380	          } else if (CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Photon) {
  381	            cPhotonTransport.TrackGmaHistory(cCalMode, cFixedSource, cCriticality, CGeometry, CMaterial, CAceData,
  382	                                             CParticleState, CTally, ORNG, cElectronTransport, cSampling);
  383	          } else if (CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Electron ||
  384	                     CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Positron) {
  385	            cElectronTransport.TrackElectronHistory(cCalMode, cFixedSource,
  386	                                                    cCriticality, CGeometry, CMaterial, CAceData,
  387	                                                    ORNG, CParticleState, CTally, cPhotonTransport, cSampling);
  388	          } else {
  389	            Output.OutputMessage([&]()
  390	            { sprintf(Output.p_sPrintStr, "Undefined particle type!.\n"); },
  391	            Output.p_fpOutputFilePtr, CDOutput::_ERROR);
  392	          }
  393	          //清理残余粒子库，这么做的原因是为了保证和WWG功能相适应
  394	          for (int particleType = 1; particleType < 4; particleType++) {
  395	            if (cFixedSource.p_vFixedParticleSrcBank[particleType].size() > 1) {
  396	              while (cFixedSource.p_vFixedParticleSrcBank[particleType].top().calculateTime == 0
  397	                     && cFixedSource.p_vFixedParticleSrcBank[particleType].size() > 1) {
  398	                cFixedSource.p_vFixedParticleSrcBank[particleType].pop();
  399	              }
  400	            }
  401	          }
  402	          if (cFixedSource.p_vFixedParticleSrcBank[CDParticleState::Neutron].size() > 1) {
  403	            cFixedSource.SampleFromParticleBank(CGeometry, CParticleState, CDParticleState::Neutron, ORNG);
  404	            CGeometry.p_OBinaryOut.UpdateParticleTypeAndHistoryNum(0, false);
  405	          } else if (cFixedSource.p_vFixedParticleSrcBank[CDParticleState::Photon].size() > 1) {
  406	            cFixedSource.SampleFromParticleBank(CGeometry, CParticleState, CDParticleState::Photon, ORNG);
  407	            CGeometry.p_OBinaryOut.UpdateParticleTypeAndHistoryNum(1, false);
  408	          } else if (cFixedSource.p_nElectronBankCount > 0) {
  409	            cFixedSource.SamplElectronBank(CGeometry, CParticleState, ORNG);
  410	            CGeometry.p_OBinaryOut.UpdateParticleTypeAndHistoryNum(static_cast<int>(CParticleState.p_eParticleType) - 1,
  411	                false);
  412	          } else {
  413	            break;
  414	          }
  415	        }
  416	        CParticleState.p_dSimulateTime = OTimer[Utility::FixedSource].interval();
  417	#ifdef USE_MPI
  418	        CTally.SumUpTally(Particle + cFixedSource.p_llParticlePosEachPro,
  419	                          CParticleState.p_dSimulateTime);   // fixed-source calculations
  420	#else
  421	        CTally.SumUpTally(Particle, CParticleState.p_dSimulateTime);
  422	#endif
  423	        if (cFixedSource.p_nFinishCalculate != -1) {
  424	          break;
  425	        }
  426	      }
  427	      cFixedSource.PrcoessBatchEnd(CTally, cNeutronTransport, CGeometry, CMaterial, CAceData, cExternalSource, cCalMode,
  428	                                   ORNG);
  429	    }
  430	  } else if (cCalMode.p_nParticleMode == CDCalMode::ElectronMode) {
  431	    while (cFixedSource.p_nFinishCalculate < 2) {
  432	      cFixedSource.DistributeSource(CGeometry, cExternalSource, ORNG);
  433	      if (cFixedSource.GetLoadBalanceMethod() != CDFixedSource::TIME_REDUCE) {
  434	        TotalCount = cFixedSource.p_llParIntervalEachPro;
  435	      }
  436	      for (long long nElectron = 1; nElectron <= TotalCount; nElectron++) {
  437	        if (LoadBalanceTimeChecker(cFixedSource, nElectron, &last_t)) {
  438	          break;
  439	        }
  440	
  441	        OTimer[Utility::FixedSource].interval();
  442	        ORNG.GetRandSeed();
  443	        ///////////////  sample source particle //////////////////////
  444	        cFixedSource.SampleFixSource(*this, cNeutronTransport, cPhotonTransport, CGeometry, CParticleState,
  445	                                     cExternalSource,
  446	                                     CAceData, CMaterial, cSampling,
  447	                                     ORNG, nElectron);
  448	        Output.CheckInputParas( CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Electron,
  449	        [&]() {sprintf(Output.p_sPrintStr, "illegal particle type( photon particle source needed in this MODE ) in source card in EXTERNSOUREC block in Photon Calculation Mode.\n");},
  450	        CDOutput::_ERROR);
  451	        if (cFixedSource.p_nFinishCalculate == 2 || cFixedSource.p_nFinishCalculate == 4) {
  452	          break;
  453	        }
  454	        CGeometry.p_OBinaryOut.UpdateParticleTypeAndHistoryNum(1, true);
  455	        cFixedSource.p_llCurParNumEachPro++;    // 这个变量是当前模拟粒子数，用于判断是否模拟完\n
  456	# ifdef USE_MPI
  457	        cFixedSource.p_llCurrentPARTICLE = cFixedSource.p_llParNumRestart + OParallel.p_nProcsNum *
  458	                                           cFixedSource.p_llCurParNumEachPro;
  459	        if (OParallel.p_bIsMaster) {
  460	          if (cFixedSource.p_llCurParNumEachPro % 1000 == 0) {
  461	            printf("electron : %lld\n", cFixedSource.p_llCurrentPARTICLE);
  462	          }
  463	        }
  464	# else
  465	        cFixedSource.p_llCurrentPARTICLE = cFixedSource.p_llParNumRestart + cFixedSource.p_llCurParNumEachPro;
  466	        if (cFixedSource.p_llCurParNumEachPro % 1000 == 0) { printf("electron : %lld\n", cFixedSource.p_llCurrentPARTICLE); }
  467	# endif
  468	
  469	        //////////////////  neutron history  /////////////////////////
  470	        for (;;) {
  471	          cElectronTransport.TrackElectronHistory(cCalMode, cFixedSource,
  472	                                                  cCriticality, CGeometry, CMaterial, CAceData,
  473	                                                  ORNG, CParticleState, CTally, cPhotonTransport, cSampling);
  474	          //清理残余粒子库，这么做的原因是为了保证和WWG功能相适应
  475	          for (int particleType = 1; particleType < 4; particleType++) {
  476	            if (cFixedSource.p_vFixedParticleSrcBank[particleType].size() > 1) {
  477	              while (cFixedSource.p_vFixedParticleSrcBank[particleType].top().calculateTime == 0
  478	                     && cFixedSource.p_vFixedParticleSrcBank[particleType].size() > 1) {
  479	                cFixedSource.p_vFixedParticleSrcBank[particleType].pop();
  480	              }
  481	            }
  482	          }
  483	          if (cFixedSource.p_nElectronBankCount > 0) {
  484	            cFixedSource.SamplElectronBank(CGeometry, CParticleState, ORNG);
  485	          } else {
  486	            break;
  487	          }
  488	        }
  489	        CParticleState.p_dSimulateTime = OTimer[Utility::FixedSource].interval();
  490	#ifdef USE_MPI
  491	        CTally.SumUpTally(nElectron + cFixedSource.p_llParticlePosEachPro,
  492	                          CParticleState.p_dSimulateTime);   // fixed-source calculations
  493	#else
  494	        CTally.SumUpTally(nElectron, CParticleState.p_dSimulateTime);
  495	#endif
  496	        if (cFixedSource.p_nFinishCalculate != -1) {
  497	          break;
  498	        }
  499	      }
  500	      cFixedSource.PrcoessBatchEnd(CTally, cNeutronTransport, CGeometry, CMaterial, CAceData, cExternalSource, cCalMode,
  501	                                   ORNG);
  502	    }
  503	  } else if (cCalMode.p_nParticleMode == CDCalMode::PhotonElectronMode) {
  504	    cFixedSource.p_vFixedParticleSrcBank[CDParticleState::Photon].push(CDFixBank());
  505	    while (cFixedSource.p_nFinishCalculate < 2) {
  506	      cFixedSource.DistributeSource(CGeometry, cExternalSource, ORNG);
  507	      if (cFixedSource.GetLoadBalanceMethod() != CDFixedSource::TIME_REDUCE) {
  508	        TotalCount = cFixedSource.p_llParIntervalEachPro;
  509	      }
  510	      for (long long Particle = 1; Particle <= TotalCount; Particle++) {
  511	        if (LoadBalanceTimeChecker(cFixedSource, Particle, &last_t)) {
  512	          break;
  513	        }
  514	
  515	        OTimer[Utility::FixedSource].interval();
  516	        ORNG.GetRandSeed();
  517	        ///////////////  sample source particle //////////////////////
  518	        cFixedSource.SampleFixSource(*this, cNeutronTransport, cPhotonTransport, CGeometry, CParticleState,
  519	                                     cExternalSource,
  520	                                     CAceData, CMaterial, cSampling,
  521	                                     ORNG, Particle);
  522	        if (cFixedSource.p_nFinishCalculate == 2 || cFixedSource.p_nFinishCalculate == 4) {
  523	          break;
  524	        }
  525	        cFixedSource.p_llCurParNumEachPro++;    // 这个变量是当前模拟粒子数，用于判断是否模拟完\n
  526	        if (CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Photon) {
  527	          CGeometry.p_OBinaryOut.UpdateParticleTypeAndHistoryNum(0, true);
  528	        } else if (CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Electron) {
  529	          CGeometry.p_OBinaryOut.UpdateParticleTypeAndHistoryNum(1, true);
  530	        }
  531	
  532	# ifdef USE_MPI
  533	        cFixedSource.p_llCurrentPARTICLE = cFixedSource.p_llParNumRestart + OParallel.p_nProcsNum *
  534	                                           cFixedSource.p_llCurParNumEachPro;
  535	        if (OParallel.p_bIsMaster) {
  536	          if (CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Photon) {
  537	            if (cFixedSource.p_llCurParNumEachPro % 1000 == 0) {
  538	              printf("photon : %lld\n", cFixedSource.p_llCurrentPARTICLE);
  539	            }
  540	          } else if (CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Electron) {
  541	            if (cFixedSource.p_llCurParNumEachPro % 1000 == 0) {
  542	              printf("electron : %lld\n", cFixedSource.p_llCurrentPARTICLE);
  543	            }
  544	          }
  545	        }
  546	# else
  547	        cFixedSource.p_llCurrentPARTICLE = cFixedSource.p_llParNumRestart + cFixedSource.p_llCurParNumEachPro;
  548	        if (CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Photon) {
  549	          if (cFixedSource.p_llCurParNumEachPro % 1000 == 0) { printf("Photon : %lld\n", cFixedSource.p_llCurrentPARTICLE); }
  550	        } else if (CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Electron) {
  551	          if (cFixedSource.p_llCurParNumEachPro % 1000 == 0) { printf("electron : %lld\n", cFixedSource.p_llCurrentPARTICLE); }
  552	        }
  553	# endif
  554	
  555	        //////////////////  Photon history  /////////////////////////
  556	        for (;;) {
  557	          if (CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Photon) {
  558	            cPhotonTransport.TrackGmaHistory(cCalMode, cFixedSource, cCriticality, CGeometry, CMaterial, CAceData,
  559	                                             CParticleState, CTally, ORNG, cElectronTransport, cSampling);
  560	          } else if (CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Electron ||
  561	                     CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Positron) {
  562	            cElectronTransport.TrackElectronHistory(cCalMode, cFixedSource,
  563	                                                    cCriticality, CGeometry, CMaterial, CAceData,
  564	                                                    ORNG, CParticleState, CTally, cPhotonTransport, cSampling);
  565	          } else {
  566	            Output.OutputMessage([&]()
  567	            { sprintf(Output.p_sPrintStr, "Undefined particle type!.\n"); },
  568	            Output.p_fpOutputFilePtr, CDOutput::_ERROR);
  569	          }
  570	          //清理残余粒子库，这么做的原因是为了保证和WWG功能相适应
  571	          for (int particleType = 1; particleType < 4; particleType++) {
  572	            if (cFixedSource.p_vFixedParticleSrcBank[particleType].size() > 1) {
  573	              while (cFixedSource.p_vFixedParticleSrcBank[particleType].top().calculateTime == 0
  574	                     && cFixedSource.p_vFixedParticleSrcBank[particleType].size() > 1) {
  575	                cFixedSource.p_vFixedParticleSrcBank[particleType].pop();
  576	              }
  577	            }
  578	          }
  579	          if (cFixedSource.p_vFixedParticleSrcBank[CDParticleState::Photon].size() > 1) {
  580	            cFixedSource.SampleFromParticleBank(CGeometry, CParticleState, CDParticleState::Photon, ORNG);
  581	          } else if (cFixedSource.p_nElectronBankCount > 0) {
  582	            cFixedSource.SamplElectronBank(CGeometry, CParticleState, ORNG);
  583	          } else {
  584	            break;
  585	          }
  586	        }
  587	        CParticleState.p_dSimulateTime = OTimer[Utility::FixedSource].interval();
  588	#ifdef USE_MPI
  589	        CTally.SumUpTally(Particle + cFixedSource.p_llParticlePosEachPro,
  590	                          CParticleState.p_dSimulateTime);   // fixed-source calculations
  591	#else
  592	        CTally.SumUpTally(Particle, CParticleState.p_dSimulateTime);
  593	#endif
  594	        if (cFixedSource.p_nFinishCalculate != -1) {
  595	          break;
  596	        }
  597	      }
  598	      cFixedSource.PrcoessBatchEnd(CTally, cNeutronTransport, CGeometry, CMaterial, CAceData, cExternalSource, cCalMode,
  599	                                   ORNG);
  600	    }
  601	  } else if (cCalMode.p_nParticleMode == CDCalMode::NeutronPhotonElectronMode) {
  602	    cFixedSource.p_vFixedParticleSrcBank[CDParticleState::Neutron].push(CDFixBank());
  603	    cFixedSource.p_vFixedParticleSrcBank[CDParticleState::Photon].push(CDFixBank());
  604	    while (cFixedSource.p_nFinishCalculate < 2) {
  605	      cFixedSource.DistributeSource(CGeometry, cExternalSource, ORNG);
  606	      if (cFixedSource.GetLoadBalanceMethod() != CDFixedSource::TIME_REDUCE) {
  607	        TotalCount = cFixedSource.p_llParIntervalEachPro;
  608	      }
  609	      for (long long Particle = 1; Particle <= TotalCount; Particle++) {
  610	        if (LoadBalanceTimeChecker(cFixedSource, Particle, &last_t)) {
  611	          break;
  612	        }
  613	
  614	        OTimer[Utility::FixedSource].interval();
  615	        ORNG.GetRandSeed();
  616	        ///////////////  sample source particle //////////////////////
  617	        cFixedSource.SampleFixSource(*this, cNeutronTransport, cPhotonTransport, CGeometry, CParticleState,
  618	                                     cExternalSource,
  619	                                     CAceData, CMaterial, cSampling,
  620	                                     ORNG, Particle);
  621	        if (cFixedSource.p_nFinishCalculate == 2 || cFixedSource.p_nFinishCalculate == 4) {
  622	          break;
  623	        }
  624	        cFixedSource.p_llCurParNumEachPro++;    // 这个变量是当前模拟粒子数，用于判断是否模拟完\n
  625	        if (CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Neutron) {
  626	          CGeometry.p_OBinaryOut.UpdateParticleTypeAndHistoryNum(0, true);
  627	        } else if (CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Photon) {
  628	          CGeometry.p_OBinaryOut.UpdateParticleTypeAndHistoryNum(1, true);
  629	        } else if (CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Electron) {
  630	          CGeometry.p_OBinaryOut.UpdateParticleTypeAndHistoryNum(2, true);
  631	        }
  632	
  633	# ifdef USE_MPI
  634	        cFixedSource.p_llCurrentPARTICLE = cFixedSource.p_llParNumRestart + OParallel.p_nProcsNum *
  635	                                           cFixedSource.p_llCurParNumEachPro;
  636	        if (OParallel.p_bIsMaster) {
  637	          if (CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Neutron) {
  638	            if (cFixedSource.p_llCurParNumEachPro % 1000 == 0) {
  639	              printf("Neutron : %lld\n", cFixedSource.p_llCurrentPARTICLE);
  640	            }
  641	          } else if (CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Photon) {
  642	            if (cFixedSource.p_llCurParNumEachPro % 1000 == 0) {
  643	              printf("Photon : %lld\n", cFixedSource.p_llCurrentPARTICLE);
  644	            }
  645	          } else if (CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Electron) {
  646	            if (cFixedSource.p_llCurParNumEachPro % 1000 == 0) {
  647	              printf("Electron : %lld\n", cFixedSource.p_llCurrentPARTICLE);
  648	            }
  649	          }
  650	        }
  651	# else
  652	        cFixedSource.p_llCurrentPARTICLE = cFixedSource.p_llParNumRestart + cFixedSource.p_llCurParNumEachPro;
  653	        if (CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Neutron) {
  654	          if (cFixedSource.p_llCurParNumEachPro % 1000 == 0) { printf("Neutron : %lld\n", cFixedSource.p_llCurrentPARTICLE); }
  655	        } else if (CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Photon) {
  656	          if (cFixedSource.p_llCurParNumEachPro % 1000 == 0) { printf("Photon : %lld\n", cFixedSource.p_llCurrentPARTICLE); }
  657	        } else if (CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Electron) {
  658	          if (cFixedSource.p_llCurParNumEachPro % 1000 == 0) { printf("Electron : %lld\n", cFixedSource.p_llCurrentPARTICLE); }
  659	        }
  660	# endif
  661	
  662	        //////////////////  neutron history  /////////////////////////
  663	        for (;;) {
  664	          if (CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Neutron) {

  666	                                      CMaterial, CAceData, CParticleState, CTally, ORNG, cSampling,
  667	                                      cPerturb);
  668	          } else if (CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Photon) {
  669	            cPhotonTransport.TrackGmaHistory(cCalMode, cFixedSource, cCriticality, CGeometry, CMaterial,
  670	                                             CAceData, CParticleState, CTally, ORNG, cElectronTransport,
  671	                                             cSampling);
  672	          } else if (CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Electron ||
  673	                     CParticleState.p_eParticleType == CDParticleState::Positron) {
  674	            cElectronTransport.TrackElectronHistory(cCalMode, cFixedSource, cCriticality, CGeometry,
  675	                                                    CMaterial, CAceData, ORNG, CParticleState, CTally,
  676	                                                    cPhotonTransport, cSampling);
  677	          } else {
  678	            Output.OutputMessage([&]()
  679	            { sprintf(Output.p_sPrintStr, "Undefined particle type!.\n"); },
  680	            Output.p_fpOutputFilePtr, CDOutput::_ERROR);
  681	          }
  682	          //清理残余粒子库，这么做的原因是为了保证和WWG功能相适应
  683	          for (int particleType = 1; particleType < 4; particleType++) {
  684	            if (cFixedSource.p_vFixedParticleSrcBank[particleType].size() > 1) {
  685	              while (cFixedSource.p_vFixedParticleSrcBank[particleType].top().calculateTime == 0
  686	                     && cFixedSource.p_vFixedParticleSrcBank[particleType].size() > 1) {
  687	                cFixedSource.p_vFixedParticleSrcBank[particleType].pop();
  688	              }
  689	            }
  690	          }
  691	          if (cFixedSource.p_vFixedParticleSrcBank[CDParticleState::Neutron].size() > 1) {

  693	          }
  694	          if (cFixedSource.p_vFixedParticleSrcBank[CDParticleState::Photon].size() > 1) {
  695	            cFixedSource.SampleFromParticleBank(CGeometry, CParticleState, CDParticleState::Photon, ORNG);
  696	          } else if (cFixedSource.p_nElectronBankCount > 0) {
  697	            cFixedSource.SamplElectronBank(CGeometry, CParticleState, ORNG);
  698	          } else {

  704	        CTally.SumUpTally(Particle + cFixedSource.p_llParticlePosEachPro,
  705	                          CParticleState.p_dSimulateTime);   // fixed-source calculations
  706	#else
  707	        CTally.SumUpTally(Particle, CParticleState.p_dSimulateTime);
  708

  710	        if (cFixedSource.p_nFinishCalculate != -1) {

  480	              }
  481	            }

  485	          } else {

  497	          break;
  498	        }
  499	      }

   38	    // 读取能群结构参数，把每个能群的中心能量和最低能量存储下来
   39	    for (int nNuc = 1; nNuc <= nTotNonSabNucNum; ++nNuc) {
   40	      // LERG = location of incident particle group structure = 1
   41	      int LERG = GetMgNeuLERG(nNuc);
   42	      for (int i = p_nNeuMltGrpNum; i >= 1; --i) {
   43	        double dCentErg = p_vNuclides[nNuc].XSS[LERG + i - 1];
   44	        double dErgWidth = p_vNuclides[nNuc].XSS[LERG + p_nNeuMltGrpNum + i - 1];
   45	        double dLowerBound = dCentErg - 0.5 * dErgWidth;
   46	        if (nNuc == 1) {
   47	          // 中心能量和最低能量这两个参数的能量存储顺序与能群编号相反
   48	          p_vNeuMltCentErg[p_nNeuMltGrpNum - i] = dCentErg;
   49	          p_vNeuMltErgBins[p_nNeuMltGrpNum - i] = dLowerBound;
   50	        } else {
   51	          // 要求所有核素的能群结构保持一致
   52	          if (fabs(p_vNeuMltErgBins[p_nNeuMltGrpNum - i] - dLowerBound) > 1.0E-6 * dLowerBound) {
   53	            Output.OutputMessage([&]() {
   54	              sprintf(Output.p_sPrintStr, "neutron multi-group structure conflicts between %s and %s.\n",
   55	                      p_vNuclides[nNuc].p_ONucID.p_chIdStr, p_vNuclides[1].p_ONucID.p_chIdStr);
   56	            },
   57	            Output.p_fpOutputFilePtr, CDOutput::_ERROR);
   58	          }
   59	        }

   60	  }

   62

   64	    int nProcess = 1;
   65	# ifdef USE_MPI

   67	# endif

   69	  } else {

   71	  }

## S023 RMC/src/Control/Control.h:18-67
   18	  class Control {
   19	  private:
   20	    /**
   21	     * @brief 计算过程中使用的hash函数
   22	     */
   23	    Utility::Hash::Func sHashFuncName = Utility::Hash::CRC64;
   24	
   25	    // todo: 1. 目前的定位方案仍然有极小的风险，主要是判断面方向时使用的是法线方向与运动方向内积的正负，而定位的时候用的是实际运动方向
   26	    //       这样有可能由于运动方向与面的夹角太小，或者虚拟步进太大，导致定位错误
   27	    //       2. 精确的方法应该是两侧使用相同的方案，如判断面的方向也使用步进方案，而非法线方向与运动方向内积的正负判断
   28	    //       这样的主要问题是计算效率可能稍有下降
   29	    //       3. 目前的设置，在边角1nm之外的范围内都是准确的，在运动方向与面方向夹角大于arc cos(1/10/10)=0.5度时应该也是准确的，
   30	    //       理论上绝大部分情况都可以处理了
   31	    /**
   32	     * @brief 计算粒子到面距离时，小于这个距离的视为在面上，利用运动方向判断其在面的哪一侧
   33	     */
   34	    double dZeroSenseDistance = 1e-8;
   35	    /**
   36	     * @brief 在定位失败后，进行虚拟步进重新定位，单次步进长度
   37	     * @note 考虑到运动方向与法线方向一般有夹角，所以数值大于距离判零的数值
   38	     *       但如果太大会导致跨度太大，可能出现定位错误
   39	     */
   40	    double dZeroMoveDistance = 10 * dZeroSenseDistance;
   41	
   42	    /**
   43	     * @brief 在定位失败后，进行虚拟步进重新定位，步进次数上限
   44	     */
   45	    int nVirtualMoveMaxCount = 10;
   46	    /**
   47	     * @brief 定位层数超过该值时开始报警告
   48	     */
   49	    int nMaxLayerNumBeforeLogging = 99;
   50	  public:
   51	    /**
   52	     * @brief 设置RMC使用的hash函数
   53	     * @param func 所设置的hash函数名称
   54	     */
   55	    void SetHashFunc(const string &func);
   56	
   57	    /**
   58	     * @brief 获取所用的hash函数
   59	     * @return 所设置的hash函数（enum）
   60	     */
   61	    inline Utility::Hash::Func GetHashFunc() { return sHashFuncName; }
   62	
   63	    inline double GetZeroMove() const { return dZeroMoveDistance; }
   64	    inline double GetZeroSenseDistance() const { return dZeroSenseDistance; }
   65	    inline int GetVirtualMoveMaxCount() const { return nVirtualMoveMaxCount; }
   66	    inline int GetMaxQuietRecurseLayerNum() const { return nMaxLayerNumBeforeLogging; }
   67	  };

   24	
   25	void CDExternalSource::NormlizeFraction() {
   26	  double dTotalFraction = 0.0;
   27	  double dTotalBiasFrac = 0.0;
   28	  //  get total fraction
   29	  //  对概率进行求和\n
   30	  for (int i = 0; i < p_vFraction.size(); ++i) {
   31	    dTotalFraction += p_vFraction[i];
   32	    //  如果没有定义BIASFRAC，则视为与真实FRACTION一致
   33	    //  偏倚概率不能是0\n
   34	    if (p_vBiasFrac[i] == 0) {
   35	      p_vBiasFrac[i] = p_vFraction[i];
   36	    }
   37	    dTotalBiasFrac += p_vBiasFrac[i];
   38	  }
   39	  //  对概率进行归一化处理\n
   40	  for (int i = 0; i < p_vFraction.size(); ++i) {
   41	    p_vFraction[i] = p_vFraction[i] / dTotalFraction;
   42	    p_vBiasFrac[i] = p_vBiasFrac[i] / dTotalBiasFrac;
   43	  }

   57	      cParticleState.p_dWgt = 1.0 * p_vFraction[i] / p_vBiasFrac[i];

## S029 RMC/src/FileIO.h:16-86
   16	class CDFileIO {
   17	public:
   18	  /**
   19	   * @brief 跳过注释和空白字符，获取文件的下一个字符，并按照预先设定的规则判断文件当前是否换行、是否结束当前card，是否文件结束。
   20	   *        Skip blanks and comments, and get next char.
   21	   * @note 该函数不支持INCLUDE构成的文件栈，如果需要跨文件读取，请调用CDInput::GetNextChar(char &NextChar)。
   22	   * @param[in, out] fptr 文件指针
   23	   * @param[in] NextChar 读出的下一个字符
   24	   * @return 当前文件状态
   25	   *        0:  Not change line
   26	   *        1:  Change one line, the new line starts with space.      -- current card is not end
   27	   *        2:  Change one line, the new line starts with non-space.  -- current card is end, but current block is not end
   28	   *        3:  Change lines, blank lines are skipped.                -- current block is end
   29	   *        4:  File end
   30	   *        todo: enum rather than integer should be used.
   31	   */
   32	  static int GetNextChar(FILE *fptr, char &NextChar) {
   33	    char s[LINE_MAX_LEN];
   34	    long lPos;
   35	    int nRead_p;
   36	    int nLineCount = 0;
   37	    int nSpaceCount = 0;
   38	    int nCommentCount = 0;
   39	    bool bFisrtSkipLineIsComment = false;
   40	
   41	    for (;;) {
   42	      lPos = ftell(fptr);
   43	      nRead_p = fscanf(fptr, "%c", &NextChar);
   44	      if (nRead_p <= 0) {
   45	        return 4;
   46	      } // 4:  file end
   47	      //  if(NextChar=='\r' || NextChar=='\n' || NextChar=='/')
   48	      if (NextChar == '\r' || NextChar == '\n' || NextChar == '/' || NextChar == '#') {
   49	        nLineCount++;
   50	        nSpaceCount = 0;
   51	        fseek(fptr, lPos, SEEK_SET);
   52	        fgets(s, LINE_MAX_LEN, fptr);
   53	        //if(NextChar=='/'){ nCommentCount++; }
   54	        if (NextChar == '/' || NextChar == '#') { nCommentCount++; }
   55	        if (nLineCount == 1) {
   56	          //if(NextChar=='/')
   57	          if (NextChar == '/' || NextChar == '#') {
   58	            bFisrtSkipLineIsComment = true;
   59	          }
   60	        }
   61	      } else if (NextChar == ' ' || NextChar == '\t') {
   62	        nSpaceCount++; // new line is a blank line beginning with space
   63	      } else if (NextChar < 0) {
   64	        char str_line[LINE_MAX_LEN];
   65	        fgets(str_line, LINE_MAX_LEN, fptr);
   66	        printf("Error: unknown character %c in input file.\n%s\nPress any key to exit...", NextChar, str_line);
   67	        exit(1);
   68	      } else {
   69	        break;
   70	      }
   71	    }
   72	    fseek(fptr, lPos, SEEK_SET);
   73	
   74	    if (nLineCount == 0) { //0:  Line not changed.
   75	      return 0;
   76	    } else if (nLineCount == 1) {
   77	      return ((nSpaceCount > 0) ? 1 : 2);
   78	    } else if (nLineCount > 1) {
   79	      if (nCommentCount == nLineCount || (!bFisrtSkipLineIsComment && nLineCount - nCommentCount == 1)) {
   80	        return ((nSpaceCount > 0) ? 1 : 2);
   81	      }
   82	      return 3;
   83	    }
   84	
   85	    printf("Error: failed reading next char.\nPress any key to exit...");
   86	    exit(1);

## S034 RMC/src/GetExitState.cpp:162-198
  162	void CDNeutronTransport::GetExitState(CDFixedSource &cFixedSource, CDAceData &cAceData, CDMaterial &cMaterial,
  163	                                      CDParticleState &cParticleState, CDRNG &ORNG, CDTally &cTally, CDCalMode &cCalMode) {
  164	  //////////////////////// multi-group case /////////////////////////////
  165	  if (cAceData.p_bIsMultiGroup) {
  166	    if (cParticleState.p_bIsAdjointParticle) {
  167	      {
  168	        //adjoint particle case
  169	        if (cParticleState.p_nColType == ReactionType::N_FISSION) {//伴随中子裂变
  170	          ////////////////////////////////// treat adjoint fission /////////////////////
  171	          if (p_bTreatFission) {//多群伴随固定源中子计算也要考虑是否裂变,如果这个选项不开则原粒子消失,等同于吸收
  172	            cFixedSource.TreatFission(*this, cAceData, cParticleState, ORNG, cTally, cCalMode);
  173	          }
  174	          cParticleState.kill();
  175	          OParticleTracker.Track(cParticleState, TER, NEUTRON,
  176	                                 NeutronFission);
  177	          OParticleTracker.checkEventsAndWrite();//WRITE_BUFFER函数只能在一代历史即将结束的时候使用
  178	        } else if (cParticleState.p_nColType == ReactionType::N_ELASTIC) {
  179	          //mt=2 伴随粒子散射事件
  180	          double MuLab;
  181	          //计算伴随粒子散射时的出射状态，注意伴随粒子能量在碰撞后是变大的,
  182	          cAceData.GetMgAdjNeuExitErgMu(cParticleState, MuLab, ORNG);
  183	          //cAceData.GetMgNeuExitErgMu(cParticleState.p_nNUC, cParticleState.p_dErg, cParticleState.p_dExitErg, MuLab,
  184	          //ORNG);
  185	          CDGlobeFun::RotateDirection(MuLab, cParticleState.p_dDir, cParticleState.p_dExitDir, ORNG);
  186	          //调整伴随粒子权重
  187	          cParticleState.p_dWgt = cParticleState.p_dWgt *
  188	                                  cParticleState.adjointAccumulatedCrossSection[cParticleState.ErgGrp() - 1] /
  189	                        (cParticleState.p_dMacroTotCs /
  190	                         cMaterial.GetMatAtomDen(cParticleState.p_nMAT, cParticleState.p_dDensRatio));
  191	          if (cParticleState.p_dExitErg <
  192	              cFixedSource.p_dMaxAdjointNeutronEnergy) { //超过粒子能量上限被杀死；能群号越小粒子能量越高
  193	            cParticleState.kill();
  194	            OParticleTracker.Track(cParticleState, TER, NEUTRON, NeutronEnergyCutoff);
  195	          }
  196	        }
  197	      }
  198	      return;

## S035 RMC/src/GetExitState.cpp:191-195

  192	              cFixedSource.p_dMaxAdjointNeutronEnergy) { //超过粒子能量上限被杀死；能群号越小粒子能量越高
  193	            cParticleState.kill();
  194	            OParticleTracker.Track(cParticleState, TER, NEUTRON, NeutronEnergyCutoff);
  195	          }

## S036 RMC/src/GetMgCs.cpp:263-278
  263	int CDAceData::LocateMgErgGrp(double dErg, int nParticleType) {
  264	  int nErgGrp = 0;
  265	  if (nParticleType == CDParticleState::eParticleType::Neutron) {
  266	    CDGlobeFun::GetIntpltPos(&p_vNeuMltErgBins[0], dErg, 0, p_nNeuMltGrpNum - 1, nErgGrp);
  267	    if (nErgGrp == 0) {
  268	      //检查粒子能量是否小于最低能群下界，若低于最低能群下界将其置于最低能群中
  269	      Output.OutputMessage([&]() {sprintf(Output.p_sPrintStr, "particle energy less than minimum energy group lower bound.\n");},
  270	      Output.p_fpOutputFilePtr, CDOutput::_WARNING);
  271	    } else if (nErgGrp == (p_nNeuMltGrpNum - 1)) {
  272	      if (dErg > (2 * p_vNeuMltCentErg[p_nNeuMltGrpNum - 1] - p_vNeuMltErgBins[p_nNeuMltGrpNum - 1])) {
  273	        //检查粒子能量是否大于最高能群上界，若高于最高能群上界将其置于最高能群中
  274	        Output.OutputMessage([&]() {sprintf(Output.p_sPrintStr, "particle energy larger than maximum energy group upper bound.\n");},
  275	        Output.p_fpOutputFilePtr, CDOutput::_WARNING);
  276	      }
  277	    }
  278	    nErgGrp = p_nNeuMltGrpNum - nErgGrp;

## S038 RMC/src/InitialBatchSource.cpp:94-144
   94	void CDFixedSource:: CalcParticleInterval(int &bCalculationEnd) {
   95	  long long llTotNumCalcAllProcessThisTime =  p_llCurParNumEachPro;
   96	  long long llParticlelNumByTime = 0;
   97	  // Get the current Sum Particle
   98	
   99	# ifdef USE_MPI
  100	  MPI_Reduce(&p_llCurParNumEachPro, &llTotNumCalcAllProcessThisTime, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG_INT, MPI_SUM, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
  101	  if (OParallel.p_bIsMaster) {
  102	
  103	# endif
  104	    p_llCurTotParNum = llTotNumCalcAllProcessThisTime + p_llParNumRestart;
  105	    p_llTimeParInterval = p_llUserInputParNum - p_llCurTotParNum;
  106	    double TotFold = 2.0;
  107	    if (Output.p_dStopTime > 0) {
  108	      TotFold = 0.5;
  109	      OTimer[Utility::Total].record();
  110	      TotFold = Output.p_dStopTime * 60 /
  111	                OTimer[Utility::Total].recordTime;  //判断计算时间是否大于用户输入时间，Output.p_dStopTime的单位是分钟，换算后计算比值//
  112	      if (TotFold <= 1) {
  113	        bCalculationEnd = true;
  114	      } else {
  115	        llParticlelNumByTime = (long long) (ceil(TotFold * llTotNumCalcAllProcessThisTime));
  116	        if ((llParticlelNumByTime + p_llParNumRestart) < p_llUserInputParNum) {
  117	          p_llTimeParInterval = llParticlelNumByTime - llTotNumCalcAllProcessThisTime;
  118	        }
  119	      }
  120	    }
  121	    // 若p_dStopTime为true且TotFold <= 1则不执行此段代码\n
  122	    if (TotFold > 1) {
  123	      // check particle number rest
  124	      p_llParNumToInputInterval = p_llUserInputParNum - p_llCurTotParNum;
  125	      //  binaryOut interval in restart block is defined
  126	      if (Output.p_llOutRestartInterval > 0) {
  127	        long long llNumPastBinaryInterval = llTotNumCalcAllProcessThisTime % Output.p_llOutRestartInterval;
  128	        p_llParNumToInputInterval = min(p_llParNumToInputInterval,
  129	                                        Output.p_llOutRestartInterval - llNumPastBinaryInterval);
  130	      }
  131	
  132	      // check particle number rest
  133	      if (p_llParNumToInputInterval == 0) { bCalculationEnd = true; }
  134	
  135	    }
  136	# ifdef USE_MPI
  137	  }
  138	
  139	  MPI_Bcast(&bCalculationEnd, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
  140	  MPI_Bcast(&p_llCurTotParNum, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
  141	  MPI_Bcast(&p_llTimeParInterval, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
  142	  MPI_Bcast(&p_llParNumToInputInterval, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
  143	# endif
  144	}

## S052 RMC/src/MeshFun.cpp:270-392
  270	void CDMesh::CalcMeshTrck(const double dPos0[3], const double dDir[3], double dTrackLen,
  271	                          vector<CDMeshTrack> &MeshTrack) {
  272	
  273	  double dPos1[3];
  274	  dPos1[0] = dPos0[0] + dDir[0] * dTrackLen;
  275	  dPos1[1] = dPos0[1] + dDir[1] * dTrackLen;
  276	  dPos1[2] = dPos0[2] + dDir[2] * dTrackLen;
  277	
  278	  MeshTrack.resize(0);
  279	
  280	  int nIndex0Xyz[3];  // start from 0
  281	  int nIndex1Xyz[3];  // start from 0
  282	  int nCrossNum = 0;
  283	  for ( int i = 0 ; i < 3 ; ++i ) {
  284	    if (p_nMeshScope[i] == SCOPE_INFINITE) {
  285	      nIndex0Xyz[i] = 0;
  286	      nIndex1Xyz[i] = 0;
  287	      continue;
  288	    }
  289	
  290	    nIndex0Xyz[i] = int(floor((dPos0[i] - p_dBoundMin[i]) / p_dMeshDelt[i])) ;
  291	    nIndex1Xyz[i] = int(floor((dPos1[i] - p_dBoundMin[i]) / p_dMeshDelt[i])) ;
  292	
  293	    nCrossNum = nCrossNum + abs(nIndex0Xyz[i] - nIndex1Xyz[i]);
  294	  }
  295	
  296	  ////////// simple case: track length is inside single mesh
  297	  if (  nCrossNum == 0) {
  298	    if (dTrackLen > 0 && IsInsideMesh(nIndex0Xyz)) {
  299	      int nMeshIndex = nIndex0Xyz[2] * p_nMeshNum[0] * p_nMeshNum[1] + nIndex0Xyz[1] * p_nMeshNum[0] + nIndex0Xyz[0] ;
  300	      MeshTrack.emplace_back(nMeshIndex, dTrackLen);
  301	    } else if (dTrackLen > 0) { //outside the mesh
  302	      MeshTrack.emplace_back(-1, dTrackLen);
  303	    }
  304	    return;
  305	  }
  306	
  307	
  308	  ////////// other case: track length crosses mesh
  309	  double dDistXyz[3];
  310	  double dWidthXyz[3];
  311	  for (int i = 0 ; i < 3 ; ++i) {
  312	    if (p_nMeshScope[i] == SCOPE_INFINITE || dDir[i] == 0) {
  313	      dDistXyz[i] = 1.0E+24;
  314	      dWidthXyz[i] = 1.0E+24;
  315	      continue;
  316	    }
  317	
  318	    if (dDir[i] > 0) {
  319	      dDistXyz[i] =  (p_dBoundMin[i] + (nIndex0Xyz[i] + 1 ) * p_dMeshDelt[i]  - dPos0[i]) / dDir[i];
  320	    } else {
  321	      dDistXyz[i] =  (p_dBoundMin[i] + nIndex0Xyz[i] * p_dMeshDelt[i]  - dPos0[i]) / dDir[i];
  322	    }
  323	
  324	    dWidthXyz[i] = fabs(p_dMeshDelt[i] / dDir[i]);
  325	
  326	  }
  327	
  328	  int n = 0;
  329	  bool bIsInside = false;
  330	  for (;;) {
  331	    /////////////// find minimum distance ////////////////
  332	    int nMinDistDim = 0;
  333	    if (dDistXyz[1] < dDistXyz[0]) {
  334	      nMinDistDim = 1;
  335	    }
  336	    if (dDistXyz[2] < dDistXyz[nMinDistDim]) {
  337	      nMinDistDim = 2;
  338	    }
  339	
  340	    double dMinDist = dDistXyz[nMinDistDim];
  341	
  342	    ///////////////////  Add mesh_index - track_len pair /////////////////
  343	    if (dMinDist > ZERO_TRACK_LENGTH) {
  344	      if (IsInsideMesh(nIndex0Xyz)) {
  345	        int nMeshIndex = nIndex0Xyz[2] * p_nMeshNum[0] * p_nMeshNum[1] + nIndex0Xyz[1] * p_nMeshNum[0] + nIndex0Xyz[0] ;
  346	        MeshTrack.emplace_back(nMeshIndex, dMinDist);
  347	        bIsInside = true;
  348	      } else {
  349	        if (bIsInside) {//从网格中飞出去，记录剩余长度
  350	          MeshTrack.emplace_back(-1, dTrackLen);
  351	          return;
  352	        } else { //尚未进入网格
  353	          MeshTrack.emplace_back(-1, dMinDist);
  354	        }
  355	      }
  356	    }
  357	
  358	
  359	    //////////////////// offset index0_xyz ///////////////////
  360	    if (dDir[nMinDistDim] > 0) {
  361	      nIndex0Xyz[nMinDistDim] = nIndex0Xyz[nMinDistDim] + 1;
  362	    } else {
  363	      nIndex0Xyz[nMinDistDim] = nIndex0Xyz[nMinDistDim] - 1;
  364	    }
  365	
  366	    //////////////////// offset dist_xyz ///////////////////
  367	    //if(dMinDist > 1.0E-8) //// bug? wrong location of index when pos0 and pos1 are on grid lines, 20140115, LIANG
  368	    //{
  369	    dTrackLen = dTrackLen - dMinDist;
  370	    for (int i = 0 ; i < 3 ; ++i) {
  371	      if (i == nMinDistDim) {
  372	        dDistXyz[i] = dWidthXyz[i];
  373	      } else {
  374	        dDistXyz[i] = dDistXyz[i] - dMinDist;
  375	      }
  376	    }
  377	    //}
  378	
  379	    ++n;
  380	    if (n >= nCrossNum) {
  381	      if (dTrackLen > ZERO_TRACK_LENGTH && IsInsideMesh(nIndex0Xyz)) {
  382	        int nMeshIndex = nIndex0Xyz[2] * p_nMeshNum[0] * p_nMeshNum[1] + nIndex0Xyz[1] * p_nMeshNum[0] + nIndex0Xyz[0] ;
  383	        MeshTrack.emplace_back(nMeshIndex, dTrackLen);
  384	      } else if (dTrackLen > ZERO_TRACK_LENGTH && !IsInsideMesh(nIndex0Xyz)) {
  385	        MeshTrack.emplace_back(-1, dTrackLen);
  386	      }
  387	      return;
  388	
  389	    }
  390	  }
  391	
  392	}

## S053 RMC/src/MeshFun.cpp:296-304
  296	  ////////// simple case: track length is inside single mesh
  297	  if (  nCrossNum == 0) {
  298	    if (dTrackLen > 0 && IsInsideMesh(nIndex0Xyz)) {
  299	      int nMeshIndex = nIndex0Xyz[2] * p_nMeshNum[0] * p_nMeshNum[1] + nIndex0Xyz[1] * p_nMeshNum[0] + nIndex0Xyz[0] ;
  300	      MeshTrack.emplace_back(nMeshIndex, dTrackLen);
  301	    } else if (dTrackLen > 0) { //outside the mesh
  302	      MeshTrack.emplace_back(-1, dTrackLen);
  303	    }
  304	    return;

## S054 RMC/src/MeshFun.cpp:474-484
  474	  ////////// simple case: track length is inside single mesh
  475	  if (nCrossNum == 0) {
  476	    if (dTrackLen > 0 && IsInsideHeterMesh(nIndex0Xyz)) {
  477	      int nMeshIndex = nIndex0Xyz[2] * p_vTotMeshNum[0] * p_vTotMeshNum[1] +
  478	                       nIndex0Xyz[1] * p_vTotMeshNum[0] + nIndex0Xyz[0];
  479	      if (p_bUseVol) {
  480	        dMeshVol = p_vMeshDelta[0][nCoarseIndex0[0]] *
  481	                   p_vMeshDelta[1][nCoarseIndex0[1]] *
  482	                   p_vMeshDelta[2][nCoarseIndex0[2]];
  483	      }
  484	      double dRealTrackLen = dTrackLen / dMeshVol;

## S055 RMC/src/MeshTallyHDF5.cpp:40-99
   40	void CDMeshTally::OutputHDF5Mesh(double const *dOriTallyData) {
   41	  CoupleMesh::StructuredMesh cTallyMesh;
   42	
   43	  // 设置网格几何信息，主要是各个网格的边界坐标 //
   44	  std::vector<std::vector<double> > vMeshBound;
   45	  vMeshBound.resize(static_cast<unsigned int>(CoupleMesh::DIMENSION));
   46	
   47	  if (!p_OTallyMesh.p_bIsHeterMesh) {
   48	    // 均匀网格还需要设置网格的数量 //
   49	    std::vector<double> vMeshNum;
   50	    vMeshNum.resize((unsigned int) CoupleMesh::DIMENSION);
   51	
   52	    for (int nDim = 0; nDim < CoupleMesh::DIMENSION; nDim++) {
   53	      auto nNumber = static_cast<unsigned int>(p_OTallyMesh.p_nMeshNum[nDim]);
   54	      vMeshNum[nDim] = (double) nNumber;
   55	      vMeshBound[nDim].resize(2);
   56	      vMeshBound[nDim][0] = p_OTallyMesh.p_dBoundMin[nDim];
   57	      vMeshBound[nDim][1] = p_OTallyMesh.p_dBoundMax[nDim];
   58	    }
   59	
   60	    cTallyMesh.SetUniformMeshBinBoundAndNum(vMeshBound, vMeshNum);
   61	  } else {
   62	    for (int nDim = 0; nDim < CoupleMesh::DIMENSION; nDim++) {
   63	      // RMC的非均匀网格分两层：外层是粗网，内层是细网：
   64	      // 粗网 -> |       |       |    |        |
   65	      // 细网 -> | | | | |   |   ||||||  |  |  |
   66	      size_t nCoarseMeshNum = p_OTallyMesh.p_vCoarseMeshSize[nDim];
   67	      for (int nCoarseIdx = 0; nCoarseIdx < nCoarseMeshNum; nCoarseIdx++) {
   68	        int nFineMeshNum = p_OTallyMesh.p_vMeshNum[nDim][nCoarseIdx];
   69	        for (int nFineIdx = 0; nFineIdx < nFineMeshNum; nFineIdx++) {
   70	          vMeshBound[nDim].push_back(
   71	              p_OTallyMesh.p_vMeshBound[nDim][nCoarseIdx] +
   72	              p_OTallyMesh.p_vMeshDelta[nDim][nCoarseIdx] * nFineIdx
   73	          );
   74	        }
   75	      }
   76	      vMeshBound[nDim].push_back(p_OTallyMesh.p_vMeshBound[nDim][nCoarseMeshNum]);
   77	    }
   78	
   79	    cTallyMesh.SetNonUniformMeshBinBound(vMeshBound);
   80	  }
   81	
   82	  // 转换RMC的Tally数据成Mesh网格数据 //
   83	  unsigned int nXSize, nYSize, nZSize;
   84	  if (!p_OTallyMesh.p_bIsHeterMesh) {
   85	    nXSize = p_OTallyMesh.p_nMeshNum[CoupleMesh::XPOS];
   86	    nYSize = p_OTallyMesh.p_nMeshNum[CoupleMesh::YPOS];
   87	    nZSize = p_OTallyMesh.p_nMeshNum[CoupleMesh::ZPOS];
   88	  } else {
   89	    nXSize = p_OTallyMesh.p_vTotMeshNum[CoupleMesh::XPOS];
   90	    nYSize = p_OTallyMesh.p_vTotMeshNum[CoupleMesh::YPOS];
   91	    nZSize = p_OTallyMesh.p_vTotMeshNum[CoupleMesh::ZPOS];
   92	  }
   93	  std::vector<std::vector<std::vector<double> > > vDataSet =
   94	      OneToThree(dOriTallyData, nXSize, nYSize, nZSize);
   95	  cTallyMesh.AppendDataset("Type" + std::to_string(tallyType->type), vDataSet);
   96	
   97	  // 输出网格文件 //
   98	  auto mesh_tally_h5 = Utility::Path::join(Output.p_sBaseDir, "MeshTally" + std::to_string(p_nTallyID));
   99	  cTallyMesh.OutputMeshFile(mesh_tally_h5);

  408	    // 尽管最好的办法是根据实际使用的模型来动态调整这两个数组的长度，

  411	    // 所以此处直接假设用户使用的模型不会超过100层，这个数值已经非常高了，

## S067 RMC/src/PrintFile.cpp:6-58
    6	void CDOutput::PrintFile(FILE *fptr, int nPrintDest, int nMssgType) {
    7	  ///////////////// Only output when MyId==0 for parallel  ///////////////////
    8	# ifdef USE_MPI
    9	  if (!OParallel.p_bIsMaster) {
   10	    if (nMssgType == CDOutput::_ERROR) {
   11	      MPI_Finalize();
   12	      exit(0);
   13	    }
   14	    return;
   15	  }
   16	# endif
   17	
   18	
   19	  //////////////// check and print destination and message type //////////////
   20	  const int nWarningMsg = 1 ;
   21	  if (nMssgType == nWarningMsg) {
   22	# ifdef USE_OMP
   23	    #pragma omp atomic
   24	# endif
   25	    p_nWarningCount++;
   26	  }
   27	
   28	  //////////////// check and print destination and message type //////////////
   29	  if (nPrintDest != _SCREEN && nPrintDest != _SCREEN_FILE && nPrintDest != _FILE) {
   30	    printf("Warning: unknown destination to print.\n");
   31	    return;
   32	  }
   33	  if (nMssgType != _NORMAL && nMssgType != _WARNING && nMssgType != _ERROR) {
   34	    printf("Warning: unknown message to print.\n");
   35	    return;
   36	  }
   37	
   38	  ///////////////// add prefix according to MssgType /////////////////////
   39	  char chPrefix[3][200] = {"", "Warning: ", "Error: "};
   40	  char *pStr = strcat(chPrefix[nMssgType], p_sPrintStr);
   41	  //strcpy(Str,Prefix[MssgType]);
   42	  /////////////////////// Print on screen/file  /////////////////////////////
   43	  if (nPrintDest == -1 ) { // only on screen
   44	    printf("%s", pStr);
   45	  } else if (nPrintDest == 1) { // only on file
   46	    fprintf(fptr, "%s", pStr);
   47	  } else { // print on both screen and file
   48	    printf("%s", pStr);
   49	    fprintf(fptr, "%s", pStr);
   50	  }
   51	  ////////////////////////// exit in case of error  ///////////////////////
   52	  if (nMssgType == 2) { //  Error. Exit
   53	    printf("\nPress ENTER to exit...\n");
   54	    //getchar();
   55	# ifdef USE_MPI
   56	    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_ERR_OTHER);
   57	# endif
   58	    exit(1);

## S070 RMC/src/RNG/StrideRNG.cpp:85-101
   85	void LCG63_0::GetRandSeed() {
   86	  static unsigned long long ullRN_GK;
   87	  static unsigned long long ullRN_CK;
   88	  static unsigned long long ullRN_ISEED;
   89	
   90	  if (RN_POSITION_PRE != (RN_POSITION - 1)) { //// only execute at the very beginning
   91	    GetSkipPara(RN_STRIDE, ullRN_GK, ullRN_CK);
   92	    unsigned long long ullGK_temp, ullCK_temp;
   93	    GetSkipPara(RN_STRIDE * RN_POSITION, ullGK_temp, ullCK_temp);
   94	    ullRN_ISEED = (((ullGK_temp * RN_SEED0) & RN_MASK) + ullCK_temp) & RN_MASK;
   95	  } else {
   96	    ullRN_ISEED = (((ullRN_GK * ullRN_ISEED) & RN_MASK) + ullRN_CK) & RN_MASK; ////
   97	  }
   98	  RN_SEED = ullRN_ISEED;
   99	  RN_POSITION_PRE = RN_POSITION;
  100	  RN_POSITION += 1;
  101	}

## S071 RMC/src/ReadControlBlock.cpp:7-43
    7	void CDInput::ReadControlBlock() {
    8	  ////////////////// check redefinition ////////////////////
    9	  map<string, bool> DefinedCard;
   10	  DefinedCard["HASH"] = false;
   11	
   12	  bool bIsBlockEnd = false;
   13	  for (;;) {
   14	    if (bIsBlockEnd) { break; }
   15	
   16	    char c;
   17	    if (GetNextChar(c) >= 3) {
   18	      break;
   19	    }
   20	
   21	    /// 1. Get the card name.
   22	    string sCard;
   23	    CDFileIO::GetKeyWord(p_fpInputFilePtr, sCard);
   24	
   25	    /// 2. Check redefinition.
   26	    Output.CheckInputParas(DefinedCard.find(sCard) != DefinedCard.end() && !DefinedCard[sCard], [&]() {
   27	      sprintf(Output.p_sPrintStr, "%s card is redefined in CONTROL block.\n", sCard.c_str());
   28	    }, CDOutput::_ERROR);
   29	    DefinedCard[sCard] = true;
   30	
   31	    /// 3. Read in the parameters in each card.
   32	    if (sCard == "HASH") {
   33	      /// 3.1 Read in the name of the hash function.
   34	      string sHashFuncName = GetStringPara(p_fpInputFilePtr);
   35	      std::transform(sHashFuncName.begin(), sHashFuncName.end(), sHashFuncName.begin(), ::toupper);
   36	      OController.SetHashFunc(sHashFuncName);
   37	    } else {
   38	      Output.OutputMessage([&]() {
   39	        sprintf(Output.p_sPrintStr, "unknown input card %s in CONTROL block.\n", sCard.c_str());
   40	      }, Output.p_fpOutputFilePtr, CDOutput::_ERROR);
   41	    }
   42	  }
   43	}

## S086 RMC/src/SampleColliType.cpp:157-197
  157	    if (cParticleState.p_bIsAdjointParticle) { //如果是多群伴随粒子情形
  158	      int mt = ReactionType::N_ELASTIC; //先默认伴随粒子发生的是散射,
  159	      int nNuc = cParticleState.p_nNUC;
  160	      int incidGrp = cParticleState.ErgGrp();//伴随粒子的实际能群编号
  161	      int exitGrp = 0;
  162	      double sampleReactionType = ORNG.Rand() *
  163	                                  cAceData.p_vNuclides[nNuc].p_vAdjointCrossSection[cParticleState.p_eParticleType][incidGrp - 1];
  164	      if (cAceData.GetMgNeuLCHI(nNuc) != 0) { //如果此种材料有裂变截面，则需要抽样伴随裂变中子
  165	        double sampleFission = cAceData.p_vNuclides[nNuc].p_vAdjointFissionCrossSection[CDParticleState::Neutron] *
  166	                               cAceData.p_vNuclides[nNuc].XSS[cAceData.GetMgNeuLCHI(nNuc) - 1 + incidGrp];
  167	        if (sampleReactionType <= sampleFission) {//fission sampled，如果此式子不成立则直接返回mt=2
  168	          sampleReactionType = sampleReactionType / cAceData.p_vNuclides[nNuc].XSS[cAceData.GetMgNeuLCHI(nNuc) - 1 + incidGrp];

## S087 RMC/src/SampleFreeFlyDist.cpp:56-96
   56	void CDNeutronTransport::CalcMacroXS(CDMaterial &cMaterial, CDAceData &cAceData, CDParticleState &cParticleState,
   57	                                     bool IsErgChanged, CDRNG &ORNG, CDSampling &cSampling, const bool bActive) {
   58	  int nCurrentMAT = cParticleState.p_nMAT;
   59	  // 重新计算截面 //
   60	  cParticleState.p_dMacroTotCs = 0;
   61	  cParticleState.p_dMacroNuFissCs = 0;
   62	  cParticleState.p_dMatMajXSForDiffTmp = 0;
   63	
   64	  // multigroup case
   65	  if (cAceData.p_bIsMultiGroup) {
   66	    for (int i = 0; i < cMaterial.GetMatTotNucNum(nCurrentMAT); ++i ) {
   67	      int nuc = cMaterial.GetMatNucIndex(nCurrentMAT, i);
   68	      int IncidGrp = cParticleState.ErgGrp();
   69	      double NucDen = cMaterial.GetMatNucAtomDen(nCurrentMAT, i, cParticleState.p_dDensRatio);
   70	#ifdef AIS
   71	      cParticleState.p_vONucCs[nuc].p_ONucCs.p_dTot = cAceData.GetMgNeuNucTotCsh5(nuc, IncidGrp);
   72	      cParticleState.p_vONucCs[nuc].p_ONucCs.p_dFis = cAceData.GetMgNucFisCsh5(nuc, IncidGrp);
   73	      cParticleState.p_vONucCs[nuc].p_ONucCs.p_dAbs = cAceData.GetMgNucAbsCsh5(nuc, IncidGrp);
   74	      cParticleState.p_vONucCs[nuc].p_ONucCs.p_dNu = cAceData.GetMgNucNubarh5(nuc, IncidGrp);
   75	#else
   76	      cParticleState.p_vONucCs[nuc].p_ONucCs.p_dTot = cAceData.GetMgNeuNucTotCs(nuc, IncidGrp);
   77	      cParticleState.p_vONucCs[nuc].p_ONucCs.p_dFis = cAceData.GetMgNucFisCs(nuc, IncidGrp);
   78	      cParticleState.p_vONucCs[nuc].p_ONucCs.p_dAbs = cAceData.GetMgNeuNucAbsCs(nuc, IncidGrp);
   79	      cParticleState.p_vONucCs[nuc].p_ONucCs.p_dNu = cAceData.GetMgNucNubar(nuc, IncidGrp);
   80	#endif
   81	      cParticleState.p_dMacroTotCs = cParticleState.p_dMacroTotCs + cParticleState.p_vONucCs[nuc].p_ONucCs.p_dTot * NucDen;
   82	      cParticleState.p_dMacroNuFissCs = cParticleState.p_dMacroNuFissCs + cParticleState.p_vONucCs[nuc].p_ONucCs.p_dNu *
   83	                                        cParticleState.p_vONucCs[nuc].p_ONucCs.p_dFis * NucDen;
   84	    }
   85	    if (cParticleState.p_bIsAdjointParticle) { //额外处理伴随总截面
   86	      cParticleState.adjointAccumulatedCrossSection.resize(cAceData.p_nNeuMltGrpNum);
   87	      cParticleState.adjointAccumulatedCrossSection.assign(cAceData.p_nNeuMltGrpNum, 0);
   88	      for (int i = 0; i < cMaterial.p_vMatSet[nCurrentMAT].p_dMatTotNucNum; i++) {//遍历本材料所有核素
   89	        for (int jh = 0; jh < cAceData.p_nNeuMltGrpNum; jh++) {
   90	          int nucPos = cMaterial.p_vMatSet[nCurrentMAT].GetMatNucIndex(i);
   91	          cParticleState.adjointAccumulatedCrossSection[jh] +=
   92	              cAceData.p_vNuclides[nucPos].p_vAdjointCrossSection[cParticleState.p_eParticleType][jh] *
   93	              cMaterial.p_vMatSet[nCurrentMAT].GetMatNucAtomDenFract(i);
   94	        }

## S098 RMC/src/SampleVariableFromDistri.cpp:6-30
    6	void CDSource::SampleVariableFromDistri(double &dWeight, bool bWithCell, CDRNG &ORNG, CDIndex &cIndex,
    7	                                        vector<CDSourceDistribution> &vDistributions) {
    8	  for (int i = 0; i < p_vLayerVarIndexDistri.size(); ++i) {
    9	    if (i == 0) {
   10	      for (int j = 0; j < p_vLayerVarIndexDistri[i].size(); ++j) {
   11	        /// 判断独立分布描述的是哪个变量，根据变量类型，抽样对应类型的变量值\n
   12	        switch (p_vLayerVarIndexDistri[i][j]) {
   13	        case WEIGHT: {
   14	          vDistributions[ p_vDistriIndex[ int(p_vLayerVarIndexDistri[i][j]) ] ].SampleValue(p_dWeight, dWeight, cIndex,
   15	              vDistributions, ORNG);
   16	          break;
   17	        }
   18	        case X: {
   19	          vDistributions[ p_vDistriIndex[ int(p_vLayerVarIndexDistri[i][j]) ] ].SampleValue(p_stXYZ.dX, dWeight, cIndex,
   20	              vDistributions, ORNG);
   21	          break;
   22	        }
   23	        case Y: {
   24	          vDistributions[ p_vDistriIndex[ int(p_vLayerVarIndexDistri[i][j]) ] ].SampleValue(p_stXYZ.dY, dWeight, cIndex,
   25	              vDistributions, ORNG);
   26	          break;
   27	        }
   28	        case Z: {
   29	          vDistributions[ p_vDistriIndex[ int(p_vLayerVarIndexDistri[i][j]) ] ].SampleValue(p_stXYZ.dZ, dWeight, cIndex,
   30	              vDistributions, ORNG);

## S104 RMC/src/SetTypeAndValues.cpp:6-34
    6	void CDSourceDistribution::SetTypeAndValues(int nDitriType, vector<double> vOptionalValue, vector<double> vProbability,
    7	    vector<double> vBias) {
    8	  // initialize p_eDistriType
    9	  p_eDistriType = eDitributionType(nDitriType);
   10	
   11	  p_vOptionalValue = vOptionalValue;
   12	  // initialize the vProbability
   13	  p_vProbability = vProbability;
   14	  // initialize the vBias
   15	  p_vBias = vBias;
   16	
   17	  CheckInitParameters();
   18	  //normalize the probability and bias
   19	  //针对几种分布，初始化分布的取值个数
   20	  if ((p_eDistriType == subDistri) || (p_eDistriType == discrete) || (p_eDistriType == bin) || (p_eDistriType == posVec)
   21	      || (p_eDistriType == piecewiseLine)) {
   22	    //区间分布，区间数等于边界值数减一
   23	    if (p_eDistriType == bin) {
   24	      p_nValueNum = vOptionalValue.size() - 1;
   25	    }
   26	    //矢量离散值分布，取值数等于输入的值数目除以3
   27	    else if (p_eDistriType == posVec) {
   28	      p_nValueNum = vOptionalValue.size() / 3;
   29	    } else {
   30	      p_nValueNum = vOptionalValue.size();
   31	    }
   32	    //对概率和偏倚概率进行归一化处理
   33	    NormalizeProbaAndBias();
   34	  }

## S105 RMC/src/SingleTally.cpp:875-898
  875	void CDMeshTally::SetupMeshTally(CDAceData &cAceData, CDTallyData &cMeshTallyData, int &nTotalMeshTallyNum) {
  876	  /////////////////// setup energy bins /////////////////
  877	  if (!p_vErgBins.empty()) {
  878	    if (p_vErgBins.size() == 1 && p_vErgBins[0] == -1) {
  879	      p_vErgBins = cAceData.p_vNeuMltErgBins;
  880	    }
  881	  }
  882	  p_nErgBinSize = static_cast<int>(p_vErgBins.size());
  883	
  884	
  885	  ///////////////////////// setup tally data size /////////////////////////
  886	  p_nTotMeshNum = p_OTallyMesh.GetTotMeshNum();  // number of meshes
  887	
  888	  p_nDataLen = p_nTotMeshNum * (p_nErgBinSize + 1);           // number of total tally bins
  889	  p_nDataStartPtr = nTotalMeshTallyNum;
  890	  nTotalMeshTallyNum += p_nDataLen;
  891	}
  892

   30	
   31	void CDTallyData::ExpdSize(int nAddedSize) { // expand size of tally data
   32	  int nOldSize = static_cast<int>(p_vSum1.size());
   33	  int nNewSize = nOldSize + nAddedSize;
   34	  p_vScore.resize(nNewSize);
   35	  p_vScoreTemp.resize(nNewSize);
   36	  p_vSum1.resize(nNewSize);
   37	  p_vSum2.resize(nNewSize);
   38	  p_vAve.resize(nNewSize);
   39	  p_vRe.resize(nNewSize);
   40	
   41	}
   42	
   43	void CDTallyData::SumTallyBin() {
   44	
   45	  for (const int &index : p_setScoreIndex) {
   46	    p_vSum1[index] = p_vSum1[index] + p_vScore[index];
   47	    double dScore2 = p_vScore[index] * p_vScore[index];
   48	    p_vSum2[index] = p_vSum2[index] + dScore2;
   49	    p_vScore[index] = 0;
   50	  }
   51	  p_setScoreIndex.clear();
   52	
   53	}
   54	
   55	void CDTallyData::SumTallyBinStride() {
   56	  for (int j = 0; j < p_vScoreIndex2.size(); ++j) {
   57	    int nStartPos = p_vScoreIndex2[j];
   58	    int n = p_vScoreStride[j];
   59	    for (int i = nStartPos; i < nStartPos + n; ++i) {
   60	      p_vSum1[i] = p_vSum1[i] + p_vScore[i];
   61	      double dScore2 = p_vScore[i] * p_vScore[i];
   62	      p_vSum2[i] = p_vSum2[i] + dScore2;
   63	      p_vScore[i] = 0;
   64	    }
   65	  }
   66	  p_setScoreIndex.clear();
   67	  p_vScoreIndex2.clear();
   68	  p_vScoreStride.clear();

## S117 RMC/src/TallyData.cpp:43-51

   44

   47	    double dScore2 = p_vScore[index] * p_vScore[index];
   48	    p_vSum2[index] = p_vSum2[index] + dScore2;
   49	    p_vScore[index] = 0;
   50	  }
   51	  p_setScoreIndex.clear();

## S118 RMC/src/TallyData.cpp:83-99
   83	void CDTallyData::CalcAveRe(double dM, double dDiv) {
   84	  /*  Note:
   85	  M; Number of observations, namely number of total batches/cycles/histories.
   86	  Div: Division. Tally average and relative error are calculated per history.
   87	  */
   88	  double dTemp2 = 1.0 / dDiv;
   89	  if (dM == 1.0) {
   90	    for (int i = 0; i < p_vSum1.size(); ++i) {
   91	      p_vAve[i] = p_vSum1[i] * dTemp2; //Sum1[i]/(M * Div);
   92	      p_vRe[i] = 0;
   93	    }
   94	  } else {
   95	    double dTemp1 = 1.0 / (dM - 1);
   96	    for (int i = 0; i < p_vSum1.size(); ++i) {
   97	      p_vAve[i] = p_vSum1[i] / dM / dDiv; //*temp2; //Sum1[i]/(M * Div);
   98	      p_vRe[i] = p_vSum1[i] == 0 ? 0 : sqrt(p_vSum2[i] / (p_vSum1[i] * p_vSum1[i]) * dM * dTemp1 - dTemp1);
   99	    }

  163	void CDFixedSource::TrackHistory(CDCalMode &cCalMode, CDNeutronTransport &cNeutronTransport,
  164	                                 CDPhotonTransport &cPhotonTransport, CDGeometry &cGeometry,

  167	  if (!cParticleState.alive()) {
  168	    // found wrong cell
  169	    return;
  170	  }
  171	  cParticleState.p_nColliCount = 0;
  172	  if (OParticleTracker.p_bPtracOpen) {
  173	    OParticleTracker.p_bPassHistory = !OParticleTracker.p_bHistoryFilterOn;
  174	    OParticleTracker.p_vSaveBuffer.clear();
  175	  }
  176	  OParticleTracker.Track(cParticleState, SRC, NEUTRON,
  177	                         NeutronSourceEvent);
  178	  cParticleState.p_nColliCount = 0;
  179	
  180	  bool bPhotonNucleus = cPhotonTransport.p_bPhotonNucleus;
  181	  if (cTally.p_bIsWithPointTally) {
  182	    cTally.TallyAtPoint(true, cGeometry, cAceData, cParticleState, cMaterial,
  183	                        true, bPhotonNucleus, ORNG, cSampling);
  184	  }
  185	  //在粒子开始输运的时候统计粒子信息
  186	  if (OWeightWindow.p_bWeightWindowGeneratorOn) {
  187	    if (cParticleState.p_nCELL == OWeightWindow.p_nRefSpace && OWeightWindow.p_eWWGtype == CDWeightWindow::CELL_BASE) {
  188	      OWeightWindow.TallyTargetCellParticleWgt(cParticleState.p_dWgt, cParticleState.p_eParticleType);
  189	    }
  190	
  191	    if (OWeightWindow.p_eWWGtype == CDWeightWindow::CELL_BASE) {
  192	      OWeightWindow.AddWgtToWgtWinGen(cParticleState.p_dErg, cParticleState.p_nCELL,
  193	                                      cParticleState.p_dWgt, cParticleState.p_eParticleType);
  194	    }
  195	  }
  196	
  197	  for (;;) {
  198	    //在粒子开始下一步输运前完成能量截断的判断。函数放这个位置有两个作用：
  199	    //1.对于刚刚抽样的源粒子而言，可以马上判断源粒子的能量是否满足能量截断的条件，避免冗余输运；
  200	    //2.可以避免在GetExitState函数中多处判断（针对不同情况），简化代码。
  201	    if (cParticleState.p_dErg <= cNeutronTransport.p_dEg0CutOff) { //energy cutoff
  202	      cParticleState.kill();
  203	      OParticleTracker.Track(cParticleState, TER, NEUTRON, NeutronEnergyCutoff);
  204	      break;
  205	    }
  206	
  207	    // save collision information
  208	    CDNeutronTransport::OutputSrcInfo(
  209	        cAceData, cParticleState, 0, static_cast<int>(p_llCurParNumEachPro));
  210	
  211	    cGeometry.p_OBinaryOut.UpdateCollisionNum(cParticleState.p_nColliCount);
  212	    // 存储粒子状态
  213	    cParticleState.backup();
  214	    // geometry tracking : free flying
  215	    RayTracking(cNeutronTransport, cGeometry, cMaterial, cAceData, cParticleState, cTally, ORNG,
  216	                cSampling, cCalMode, cPerturb);
  217	
  218	    /// 使用碰撞估计器统计计数(认为在ray-tracing后，粒子已经发生了碰撞，而对应的最大材料截面，在抽样粒子自由程时已经计算)
  219	    cTally.TallyByCollision(true, cGeometry, cAceData, cParticleState, cMaterial, ORNG,
  220	                            cSampling, nullptr);
  221	
  222	    if (!cParticleState.alive()) {
  223	      break;
  224	    }
  225	
  226	    // sample collision nuclide
  227	    CDNeutronTransport::SampleColliNuc(cMaterial, cAceData, cParticleState, ORNG);
  228	    if (!cParticleState.alive()) {
  229	      break;
  230	    }

  232	    // TMS的靶核速度抽样与拒绝抽样\n
  233	    if (cAceData.p_bTMS) {

  235	      if (cParticleState.p_bTMSReject) {
  236	        continue;
  237	      }
  238	    }
  239	
  240	    // calculate cross-sections
  241	    cNeutronTransport.CalcColliNucCs(cMaterial, cAceData, cParticleState, ORNG, cPerturb, true);

    6	void CDNeutronTransport::TreatImpliCapt(CDAceData &cAceData, CDParticleState &cParticleState, CDGeometry &cGeometry,
    7	                                        CDRNG &ORNG, bool bAbsIncludeFis) const {
    8	  //in adjoint calculation implicit capture is not considered.固定源伴随粒子输运不考虑隐俘获
    9	  if (cAceData.p_bIsMultiGroup && cParticleState.p_bIsAdjointParticle) { return; }

   12	                          cParticleState.p_OColNucCs.p_dFis * int(bAbsIncludeFis)) / cParticleState.p_OColNucCs.p_dTot);

## S125 RMC/src/Utility/Timer.h:82-99
   82	    double interval() {
   83	      if (running) {
   84	        std::chrono::duration<double> diff = clock::now() - startTime;
   85	        double dtime = recordTime;
   86	        recordTime = diff.count();
   87	        return (recordTime - dtime);
   88	      } else {
   89	        return -1;
   90	      }
   91	    }

   95	     */
   96	    void reset() {
   97	      runTime = 0.0;
   98	      running = false;
   99	    };

## S127 RMC/src/WeightWindows.cpp:45-66
   45	void CDWeightWindow::setMeshWeightWindowBound(double &dLow, double &dSurvive, double &dUpper, int particleType,
   46	    const int &meshIndex, const double &erg) {
   47	  int ergPos = 0;
   48	  CDGlobeFun::GetIntpltPos(&OWeightWindow.p_vEnergyBins[particleType][0], erg,
   49	                           0, int(OWeightWindow.p_vEnergyBins[particleType].size()) - 1, ergPos);
   50	  if (erg > OWeightWindow.p_vEnergyBins[particleType][OWeightWindow.p_vEnergyBins[particleType].size() - 1]) {
   51	    ergPos = OWeightWindow.p_vEnergyBins[particleType].size() - 2;
   52	  }
   53	#ifdef USE_MPI
   54	  if (p_bUseSharedMPIWeightWindow) {
   55	    int displacement =
   56	        p_vParticleTypeMeshDisplc[particleType] +
   57	        (int(p_vEnergyBins[particleType].size()) - 1) * meshIndex
   58	        + ergPos;
   59	    dLow = SharedMemoryPosition[displacement].p_dLowerWeightBound;
   60	    dSurvive = SharedMemoryPosition[displacement].p_dSurvivalWeight;
   61	    dUpper = SharedMemoryPosition[displacement].p_dUpperWeightBound;
   62	  } else {
   63	#endif
   64	    dLow = p_vMeshInformation[particleType][meshIndex][ergPos].p_dLowerWeightBound;
   65	    dSurvive = p_vMeshInformation[particleType][meshIndex][ergPos].p_dSurvivalWeight;
   66	    dUpper = p_vMeshInformation[particleType][meshIndex][ergPos].p_dUpperWeightBound;

## S128 RMC/src/WeightWindows.cpp:45-101
   45	void CDWeightWindow::setMeshWeightWindowBound(double &dLow, double &dSurvive, double &dUpper, int particleType,
   46	    const int &meshIndex, const double &erg) {
   47	  int ergPos = 0;
   48	  CDGlobeFun::GetIntpltPos(&OWeightWindow.p_vEnergyBins[particleType][0], erg,
   49	                           0, int(OWeightWindow.p_vEnergyBins[particleType].size()) - 1, ergPos);
   50	  if (erg > OWeightWindow.p_vEnergyBins[particleType][OWeightWindow.p_vEnergyBins[particleType].size() - 1]) {
   51	    ergPos = OWeightWindow.p_vEnergyBins[particleType].size() - 2;
   52	  }
   53	#ifdef USE_MPI
   54	  if (p_bUseSharedMPIWeightWindow) {
   55	    int displacement =
   56	        p_vParticleTypeMeshDisplc[particleType] +
   57	        (int(p_vEnergyBins[particleType].size()) - 1) * meshIndex
   58	        + ergPos;
   59	    dLow = SharedMemoryPosition[displacement].p_dLowerWeightBound;
   60	    dSurvive = SharedMemoryPosition[displacement].p_dSurvivalWeight;
   61	    dUpper = SharedMemoryPosition[displacement].p_dUpperWeightBound;
   62	  } else {
   63	#endif
   64	    dLow = p_vMeshInformation[particleType][meshIndex][ergPos].p_dLowerWeightBound;
   65	    dSurvive = p_vMeshInformation[particleType][meshIndex][ergPos].p_dSurvivalWeight;
   66	    dUpper = p_vMeshInformation[particleType][meshIndex][ergPos].p_dUpperWeightBound;
   67	#ifdef USE_MPI
   68	  }
   69	#endif
   70	}
   71	
   72	double CDWeightWindow::GetWeightWindowEnergy(CDAceData &cAceData,
   73	                                               const CDParticleState &cParticleState) const {
   74	  if (p_bUseMCNPweightwindow) {
   75	    return cParticleState.p_dErg;
   76	  }
   77	  return cAceData.GetErgValue(cParticleState.p_dErg, cParticleState.p_eParticleType);
   78	}
   79	void CDWeightWindow::ProcessWeightWindow(CDGeometry &cGeometry, const int particleType, vector<double> &ErgBins,
   80	    vector<double> &WeightWindowParameter, double WUPN, double WSURVN) {
   81	  int totalWeightWindowParaNum = WeightWindowParameter.size();
   82	  int ergBinNum = int(ErgBins.size()) - 1;
   83	  int spatialNum = p_OWeightWindowMesh.GetTotMeshNum();
   84	  Output.CheckInputParas(totalWeightWindowParaNum == spatialNum * ergBinNum,
   85	  [&]() {
   86	    sprintf(Output.p_sPrintStr,
   87	            "WWMESH parameter count (%d) does not match mesh count (%d) times energy-bin count (%d).\n",
   88	            totalWeightWindowParaNum, spatialNum, ergBinNum);
   89	  }, CDOutput::_ERROR);
   90	  p_vMeshInformation[particleType].resize(spatialNum);//初始化带有权窗的几何的数目;
   91	  for (int i = 0; i < spatialNum; i++) {
   92	    p_vMeshInformation[particleType][i].resize(ergBinNum);
   93	    for (int j = 0; j < ergBinNum; j++) {
   94	      p_vMeshInformation[particleType][i][j].p_dLowerWeightBound = WeightWindowParameter[i * ergBinNum +
   95	          j];
   96	      p_vMeshInformation[particleType][i][j].p_dUpperWeightBound =
   97	          WUPN * p_vMeshInformation[particleType][i][j].p_dLowerWeightBound;
   98	      p_vMeshInformation[particleType][i][j].p_dSurvivalWeight =
   99	          WSURVN * p_vMeshInformation[particleType][i][j].p_dLowerWeightBound;
  100	    }
  101	  }

## S129 RMC/src/WeightWindows.cpp:72-77
   72	double CDWeightWindow::GetWeightWindowEnergy(CDAceData &cAceData,
   73	                                               const CDParticleState &cParticleState) const {
   74	  if (p_bUseMCNPweightwindow) {
   75	    return cParticleState.p_dErg;
   76	  }
   77	  return cAceData.GetErgValue(cParticleState.p_dErg, cParticleState.p_eParticleType);

   80	    vector<double> &WeightWindowParameter, double WUPN, double WSURVN) {
   81	  int totalWeightWindowParaNum = WeightWindowParameter.size();
   82	  int ergBinNum = int(ErgBins.size()) - 1;
   83	  int spatialNum = p_OWeightWindowMesh.GetTotMeshNum();
   84	  Output.CheckInputParas(totalWeightWindowParaNum == spatialNum * ergBinNum,
   85	  [&]() {
   86	    sprintf(Output.p_sPrintStr,
   87	            "WWMESH parameter count (%d) does not match mesh count (%d) times energy-bin count (%d).\n",
   88	            totalWeightWindowParaNum, spatialNum, ergBinNum);
   89	  }, CDOutput::_ERROR);
   90	  p_vMeshInformation[particleType].resize(spatialNum);//初始化带有权窗的几何的数目;
   91	  for (int i = 0; i < spatialNum; i++) {
   92	    p_vMeshInformation[particleType][i].resize(ergBinNum);
   93	    for (int j = 0; j < ergBinNum; j++) {
   94	      p_vMeshInformation[particleType][i][j].p_dLowerWeightBound = WeightWindowParameter[i * ergBinNum +
   95	          j];
   96	      p_vMeshInformation[particleType][i][j].p_dUpperWeightBound =
   97	          WUPN * p_vMeshInformation[particleType][i][j].p_dLowerWeightBound;
   98	      p_vMeshInformation[particleType][i][j].p_dSurvivalWeight =
